**2026-07-16 - E8 PRICING-MECHANISM ANALYSIS: COMPLETE. The source's ASYMMETRY claim is CONFIRMED and survives intact (ASSERTED, 13-21 sigma). Its ATTRIBUTION does not: the +$10,142 headline belongs to REACTING AT ALL, and the formula's ceiling in that same environment is 0.54% of it. Recorded as VERDICT (Claim B) + ESTIMATE-WITH-BOUNDS (Claim A) + stability statement (robustness). Freeze 25a8dbd; Stage 1 suite ALL PASS (7 legs); analysis run ~30s; DISC-01, DISC-02, DISC-03, DISC-04 all now RESOLVED.**

WHAT E8 IS. An ANALYSIS of the source's own committed artifact - 1,800 raw trial records, SHA256-registered in SOURCES.md and asserted before any arithmetic - not a re-execution. That rests on verification earned, not assumed: the source's pricing code clears ALL SEVEN CIC classes (DESIGN 16c); its closure is vendored at analysis/vendor/ and MD5-asserted (16d); the artifact's hash pins every finding to specific bytes. A re-run was MEASURED and rejected (16e): 19.04 s/trial, so five environments x four scenarios costs 5.3h at 50 seeds and 55.5h at the 526 needed to resolve a +13.01 effect - and powering to +13.01 is CIRCULAR, since +13.01 is itself a 0.60-sigma reading, so powering to it assumes it is real. A 60-seed run was proposed and rejected on arithmetic: SE scales as sqrt(50/60) = 0.913, no cell changes status. THE FINDING IS A BOUND AND THEIR 50 SEEDS ALREADY CARRY IT.

CLAIM B - THE ASYMMETRY OF PRICING REACTION (naive_reactive vs no_pricing): VERDICT ASSERTED against the frozen rule. Raises positive in the capacity-strained sustained-upward environment: +10141.86 [+8906.72, +11377.00], 16.1 sigma. Cuts negative in EVERY downward environment: level_shift_down_persistent -2315.25 (18.9 sigma), low_phi_shift_down -581.36 (17.1 sigma), mid_phi_shift_down -1238.29 (20.9 sigma). Also low_phi_shift_up +1646.78 (13.1 sigma). All five resolved. THE SOURCE'S ASYMMETRIC FINDING IS REAL, STRONGLY EVIDENCED, AND NEEDS NO REFRAMING. Standard operations intuition ("cut prices to hold volume when demand falls") is wrong in a capacity-strained chain, and the source demonstrated it properly.

CLAIM A - THE FORMULA'S VALUE (phi_gated_asymmetric vs naive_reactive): reported as a BOUND per environment, in absolute terms and as a percentage of that environment's own Claim B headline.
  level_shift_up_persistent    +13.01 [ -29.17,  +55.18]  0.6 sig  =  [-0.29%,  +0.54%] of +10141.86
  low_phi_shift_up             +19.42 [  +2.76,  +36.09]  2.3 sig  =  [+0.17%,  +2.19%] of  +1646.78  RESOLVED
  level_shift_down_persistent  -24.28 [ -62.22,  +13.66]  1.3 sig  =  [-2.69%,  +0.59%] of  -2315.25
  low_phi_shift_down          +137.20 [ +75.65, +198.76]  4.4 sig  = [+13.01%, +34.19%] of   -581.36  RESOLVED
  mid_phi_shift_down           -24.55 [ -71.95,  +22.85]  1.0 sig  =  [-5.81%,  +1.85%] of -1238.29
IN THE ENVIRONMENT CARRYING THE PAPER'S HEADLINE, THE FORMULA CANNOT BE CONTRIBUTING MORE THAN 0.54% OF WHAT IT IS CREDITED WITH. That is not "we could not tell" - it is a hard ceiling at 95% confidence. THE BOUND IS THE FINDING; three cells not resolving is irrelevant, because resolution was never the question. The formula's ONE genuine, resolved win is low_phi_shift_down at 13-34% of that environment's value - the case the paper never mentions.

DISC-03 RESOLVED - THE ATTRIBUTION DEFECT, CONFIRMED AND MEASURED. v16 frames its pricing section as testing whether THE PERSISTENCE FORMULA gives useful guidance, and reports +$10,142/period as the evidence. That figure is no_pricing vs NAIVE_REACTIVE - the value of reacting to demand shifts at all. The source's OWN CODE says so, in PricingPolicy's docstring: "Comparing against this baseline [naive] ISOLATES THE VALUE OF THE FORMULA'S PERSISTENCE-DISCRIMINATION CAPABILITY beyond the value of dynamic pricing in general." THEY BUILT THE RIGHT COMPARATOR AND REPORTED THE WRONG ONE. Their record also documents the mechanism: OLS structural-break inflation pushes the persistence estimate above BOTH thresholds (0.60 and 0.75) in the high-persistence environments, so both gates always open and the gate never gates. This is the Paper-4 ratio-operator pattern: reproducible, and inadmissible for the sentence attached to it. It is INDEPENDENT of DISC-01/02 - it holds even though every number is correct, and CIC-1 confirms every number IS correct.

THE ROBUSTNESS LEGS - THE SHARPEST PART, and the paper does not carry them. CIC-1 EXTENDS: our own code, on their capacity artifacts, reproduces v16's capacity claims EXACTLY. Up-benefit by capacity: 1.3x +10141.86; 1.8x +842.20; 2.4x +776.74; 3.0x +775.15. v16 says "under $900 at 1.8x" -> +842.20. v16 says "collapses by 12x at 1.8x" -> 10141.86/842.20 = 12.04x. BOTH EXACT. This independently confirms DISC-01 from their DATA through OUR CODE, not merely from their record: the supporting draft's $5,200 at 1.8x is fiction; v16 transcribed faithfully.
  IT IS A CLIFF, NOT A GRADIENT: 10141.86 -> 842.20 between 1.3x and 1.8x, then FLAT (842 -> 777 -> 775). The tool's pricing value lives entirely in a narrow band of genuine capacity strain.
  THE NET ASYMMETRY FLIPS WITH CAPACITY - the finding the paper omits. Down-harm falls only ~40% across the range (-2315.25 -> -1374.46, which is exactly the source's own "40% reduction across capacity range") while up-benefit collapses 12x. Net by capacity: 1.3x +10142 vs -2315 = NET WINNER; 1.8x +842 vs -1400 = NET LOSER; 2.4x +777 vs -1376 = NET LOSER; 3.0x +775 vs -1374 = NET LOSER. ABOVE TIGHT CAPACITY, SYMMETRIC PRICING LOSES MONEY. Every cell resolved at 15-26 sigma. The source's own record flagged this as candidate "finding ten"; the paper does not carry it. CAVEAT, stated: "net" presumes a business faces upward and downward shifts at comparable rates - that is an assumption about the world, not a measurement. The COMPONENTS are measured; the netting is a framing.
  ELASTICITY: benefit robust (0.5 -> +10940.67; 1.5 -> +10141.86; 3.0 -> +9956.76, a 10% spread), harm strongly elasticity-sensitive (low_phi_shift_down: -1223.92 / -581.36 / -398.56, a 3x range). Price cuts hurt MOST when demand is inelastic - cuts destroy margin without buying volume.

A DEFECT OF OURS: the robustness legs COMPUTED but did not PRINT - main() writes them to JSON and never displays them, while the pre-registered reporting commitment requires the capacity leg reported. Caught at the stop-and-review, extracted by a separate command. An output defect, not an analysis defect; the numbers were correct and complete in the artifact. Recorded because it is the leg carrying the genuinely new information, and a reporting commitment that is not honoured by the reporting code is a live hazard.

METHOD FAILURES RECORDED AGAINST OURSELVES (E8 produced four, all one disease). (1) The magnitude check against E4 as the reference system - E4 is a single-product 4-echelon chain, this is 12 SKUs summed x6; wrong yardstick. (2) The cumulative/relabel hypothesis for the dollar figures, raised TWICE and wrong twice, both times resting on (1). (3) DISC-04's seed/environment suspicion - both limbs wrong: the runner hands stockpyl a deterministic demand list so rand_seed=42 is inert, and ar1_high/ar1_high_no_shift are one no-shift control under two names. (4) The severity claim that phi_gated is bit-identical to naive - true ONLY in that same no-shift control, which I read as the headline environment; the arms are distinct in all five level-shift environments. IN EVERY CASE THE ANOMALY WAS REAL AND THE DIAGNOSIS WAS INVENTED. Note the specific trap: (4) is the SAME naming duplication as (3), one dossier later - the lesson did not transfer because the second instance arrived wearing different clothes. THE OPERATIVE RULE: a pattern of identical values is a signal to OPEN THE RAW RECORDS, never evidence of what caused it.

WHAT E8 MAY AND MAY NOT CLAIM. MAY: we audited the source's pricing implementation against the 7-point CIC (all seven clear), vendored its closure unmodified and MD5-asserted, registered its artifact by SHA256, and analysed its 1,800 raw records to compute both claims separately. MAY NOT: claim independent re-implementation, or claim to have re-run anything. L-03 governs: this binds the MODEL, not the world.

RAMIFICATIONS - the pricing section needs THREE changes, not one. (1) The ASYMMETRY CLAIM STANDS: keep it, it is well-evidenced at 13-21 sigma and it contradicts standard operations intuition, which is the paper's most defensible contribution here. (2) THE ATTRIBUTION IS WITHDRAWN: the +$10,142 is the value of reacting at all; the formula's ceiling in that environment is 0.54% of it. The honest replacement claim is available and better: the formula's demonstrated domain is low_phi_shift_down (13-34% of that environment's value, resolved) - noisy demand falling, where the gate correctly refuses to cut. (3) THE CAPACITY CONDITION IS A CLIFF AND THE PRODUCT IS NET-NEGATIVE ABOVE IT: pricing value exists only at ~1.3x strain; at 1.8x and looser the downward harm exceeds the upward benefit. LB-E8-up and LB-E8-down land. ARG-19's E8 leg lands.

EIGHT EXPERIMENTS: E1 VERDICT SUPPORT (falsifier) - E2 VERDICT SUPPORT + mechanism - E3 CHARACTERIZATION (boundary) - E4 VERDICT SUPPORT (model) + ESTIMATE (see DISC-06, DISC-07) - E5 CHARACTERIZATION - E6 CHARACTERIZATION + ESTIMATE - E7 CHARACTERIZATION (source confirmed and sharpened; effect conditional on capacity) - E8 VERDICT (Claim B ASSERTED) + ESTIMATE-WITH-BOUNDS (Claim A <=0.54% of its headline). A THIRD-TIME PATTERN, worth naming: in E7 the omitted finding (ar1_moderate benefits in all nine cells) was stronger than the headline; in E8 the omitted finding (net-negative above 1.3x) is more decisive than the headline. The source consistently under-reports its own best and worst results.

LADDER (playbook order of attack; every claim read from an artifact, never a summary; the pin MD5 re-verified 93135760b92cc195da36eb3c2b785ded before v16 was read): (8) framing gate -> (9) triage -> (10) version reconciliation -> (1) source recovery to exhaustion -> (3) intermediates (negative) -> (7) cross-validation against the as-proven theorem. THE THREE-PART RESOLUTION. E4 implemented 5.4's two-tier structure; E7 the corrected sweep. They were never supposed to match. (ii) E4'S BUILD DEFECTS (reconstruction partially in error): BG_POLICY = 0.9561 reverse-engineered from the draft's "~0.83 boundary" - which is REAL but belongs to the SWEEP: it is the closed-form implicit gate, 0.90 x (pi^2/2)/S(phi,8) = 1 at phi = 0.8216 (verified; and rho(0.83, 8, 0.9561) = 1.000001 confirms the back-derivation) - overriding Appendix E's stated "base beta*gamma = 0.50"; the operator's ERP comparator (0.50 gap closure - confirmed by Appendix E AND by make_erp_team in the archived script, alpha pinned at 0.50) was NEVER BUILT, so the verdict was rendered against full-gap self-calibrating base-stock; Naive was never built (DISC-06 - bit-identical duplicate; OUTLINE's "capacity binds equally" was a FABRICATED mechanism, withdrawn); the full tier omits 5.4's drift detection. (iii) SECTION 5.4'S ORIGINAL IS UNRECOVERABLE AND THEOREM-INCOHERENT: the $60.9M/$7.5M/$5.2M/$5.1M quartet appears verbatim in the 04-20 Strategic Plan - TWO DAYS BEFORE the source's own threshold correction - the generating script exists NOWHERE on disk (Werner tree + Archive, LaggingTruth mirror, all April date folders, LaggingTruth\Archive, C:\ResearchShare - exhausted), and at the stated parameters (bg = 0.50, W = 8) the as-proven theorem says the tool NEVER ENGAGES: rho = 1 boundary at phi = 0.9990 (numerical, T1-verified rho) and analytically bg x S > pi^2/2 requires S > 9.87, impossible at W = 8 where S <= 8. Verified TWO independent ways. The ~30%-vs-ERP improvement can only have come from the pre-correction rho implementation the source itself repudiated. ORIGINAL UNRECOVERABLE.

TWO CORRECTIONS TO THE 2026-07-13 RECORD, both traced to reading the corrupted draft instead of the pinned source: (a) "the gap-closure 0.50 in the DESIGN operator was the SPEC-B monitor parameter, never the policy's ordering gain" - DISPROVEN: Appendix E lists gap closure 0.50 (ERP's gain) and base beta*gamma 0.50 (spectral tools') as TWO separate parameters; (b) "the abstract's ~30% is vs the Sterman worst-case baseline" - FALSE: v16 5.4 states it is Algorithm 3 vs Algorithm 2 (Modern ERP), arithmetic checks (7.5M -> 5.2M = 30.7%); the 88-95% Sterman figures belong to the sweep record. SCIENCE - ALL 8 CELLS RESOLVED at 20 seeds. Strained sticky (level_shift_up_persistent): +8401.30 (h=0) -> +8628.03 (0.1) -> +7808.21 (0.3) -> +5855.08 (0.6, 6.2 sigma) - the raise benefit retains ~70% of baseline after PERMANENTLY LOSING 55% OF THE CUSTOMER BASE (pool 0.452); the downgrade trigger (resolved-negative at h=0.3) was nowhere near firing (+7808 at 8.7 sigma). Noisy (low_phi_shift_up): +1407.26 -> +496.37 (2.3 sigma) -> -540.58 (-2.4 sigma) -> -3320.45 (-12.1 sigma) - a RESOLVED benefit-to-harm crossing bracketed [0.1, 0.3]; at heavy hysteresis reacting is decisively WORSE than doing nothing. DECISION RULE (frozen, executed mechanically): RETAIN-WITH-SPLIT-FRAMING.

MECHANISM OBSERVED, NOT ASSUMED: pool erosion is essentially IDENTICAL across environments at each h (0.772/0.770, 0.663/0.672, 0.452/0.452), yet the benefits diverge by thousands of dollars - attrition alone does not decide the outcome. What decides it: whether the raise answers a REAL persistent shift under binding capacity (the shed customers largely could not have been served anyway; margin does the work) or a noisy non-signal (full attrition cost paid for unwarranted raises). That contrast IS the split framing - observed in the cells rather than assumed in the prose. Comparator arm proven inert in the run as in the artifact (pool = 1.0, zero changes, every no_pricing trial). Claim-B family throughout; nothing attributable to the formula (DISC-03 gate holds). Caveat as found: hp h=0.1 point estimate exceeds h=0 (+8628 vs +8401), within noise, reported not interpreted.

ADJUDICATION CAPACITY, confirmed both directions: severity was pre-measured at the gate (the downgrade trigger sat at 8.7 sigma resolution - a fragile finding would have registered unambiguously); the instrument engaged (pools to 0.45); no saturation, no dead dial. Standing limits: n = 20 (the source's own; every cell resolved regardless; thinnest lp@0.1 at 2.3 sigma); first-order one-directional multiplicative attrition model (disclosed choice); L-03 binds the model.

MANUSCRIPT CONSEQUENCES: (a) the practitioner guidance MUST carry the split - raising into a genuine persistent surge under tight capacity is robust even to heavy permanent customer loss, while the same reflex in a noisy environment turns actively harmful at quite moderate hysteresis; (b) Appendix F's hysteresis paragraph is re-earned in full by our code and gains the mechanism sentence and the resolved crossing bracket; (c) cuts-are-bad is untouched by construction (hysteresis never engages at/below reference - stated, not tested, as pre-registered); (d) the boundary map (hp: no crossing in range; lp: resolved crossing [0.1, 0.3]) becomes a reportable element of TBL-7.

**2026-07-25 - MISSING METHODS SECTION FOUND; ROOT-CAUSED; STANDARD AMENDED TO v1.9.10 (no manuscript change yet - Paper 9 remediation is authorized-pending).** THE FINDING: a mid-phase re-read of the Phase-4 checklist (per the checklist-first rule) established that the Standard's IMRaD run requires a METHODS section, that it is not bracketed-optional, and that this manuscript has none - Section 4 (theorem) runs straight into Section 5 (Empirical Validation). ROOT CAUSE, traced through the ClickUp phase tasks rather than guessed: (a) the omission entered at PHASE 1 - OUTLINE v0.1's own changelog records the roadmap as "built from the pinned v16 source", the source has no Methods section, and only Appendix G is marked NEW, so the IMRaD map transcribed the source's section list; (b) Phase 1's checkbox enumerates what the outline must CONTAIN (argument, citations, findings, figures/tables/equations, scope, conclusions, cross-references) but never that its section run must match the Standard's - the Methods requirement appears at Phase 1 only in the task's Output PROSE ("this is where the paper's skeleton and Methods section are born"), which is not a box and not machine-checked; (c) COVERAGE could not catch it BY CONSTRUCTION - 96 rows, ZERO DROP, both directions run, but COVERAGE is a source-to-rebuild check and a section the source never had is invisible to it, so the rebuild path silently promotes the pinned source from STARTING POINT to SPECIFICATION; (d) the reconciliation gate could not catch it EITHER - it diffs manuscript against OUTLINE, both omit Methods, both agree, GREEN. A gate comparing two artifacts is structurally blind to an omission they SHARE, which is the v1.6 principle ("never rely on noticing absence - enumerate and diff") failing one level up: we enumerated the outline's elements and never enumerated the required SECTIONS; (e) the one box that names Methods, Phase-4 box 1, is explicitly deferred ("flips when the sections carry drafted prose, not seeds"), so the structural check was scheduled for AFTER all drafting, when renumbering the whole back half is most expensive - I read that box at phase open and read its deferral as "not yet actionable" rather than separating the structural claim from the prose claim, which is my own contribution to the delay. PRECEDENT, MISSED: MSC's Methods 3.4 was CREATED during the June 2026 structural-parity refactor (four cross-references rewritten to point at it) and filed as a layout-parity chore rather than a checklist gap, so no rule followed - two of nine papers, both the ones whose sources lacked the section. STANDARD v1.9.10 SHIPPED (anti-bloat: the machine check is the enforcement, the rest only prevents the reasoning error that produced it): verify.py check (5) now asserts a REQUIRED SECTION RUN (Abstract, Introduction, Methods, Results, Discussion, Conclusion, References; literature/related-work whenever prior-art is cited) from a FIXED list and NOT from the outline's own map, firing at the Phase-4 OPEN run when a missing section costs one heading; OUTLINE Template gains the required-run reconciliation item placed ABOVE the pre-existing "the IMRaD map matches the manuscript's actual section list" - the exact line that made the map the specification - plus the fixed-list independence in "What the gate checks"; the Standard's Outline rule and the Phase-1/Phase-4 Done lines carry the required run; Phase 1 gains one clause (COVERAGE proves nothing was LOST, never that the rebuild is COMPLETE); template task list mirrored in the same edit per change-management (Phase-1 outline item + rebuild clause; Phase-4 box 1 SPLIT into a structural half checked at skeleton seed and a prose half checked at close); Change History entry recorded. ClickUp task 86bb3fwbb opened for the check's implementation, Paper 9's remediation, the eight-paper audit, and the re-check-or-grandfather decision per frozen repo. **PROCESS DEFECT, MINE, LOGGED:** while making these edits I sent a PARTIAL payload to the OUTLINE Template page - a document-page update REPLACES the whole page, so the title, artifact rules, metadata, changelog, and all seven content sections were deleted. The read-back is the control, never the tool's success message. VERIFY GREEN all six at 437 rows on every one; RENDER GREEN 512/437 unchanged throughout - prose adds no tokens; every citation key and every C-01..C-06 / L-01..L-07 anchor conserved through the rewrites (the paper check is bidirectional on citations and greps the C-/L- ids, so a dropped one REDs - this is what makes wholesale prose rewriting safe). SHAPE: rendered 15,186 -> 15,784 -> 16,562 -> 16,911 words against the pinned source's 17,456, i.e. -13.0% -> -9.6% -> -5.1% -> -3.1%. The trajectory from -21.9% before Methods to -3.1% now settles box 13 in advance: below the 10% recorded-check line, far below the 15% sign-off line, with experiments 15 v 15, citations 74 v 70, and proofs UP (three theorems plus the k* proposition with full written proofs where the source carried sketches). SUBSTANCE: 2.1 rewritten from a citation dump to state what the steady-state tradition ASSUMES AWAY - it analyses at a GIVEN phi and therefore says nothing about the interval in which phi has changed and the estimate has not - with the two nearest competitors positioned individually rather than lumped; 2.3 explains why semiconductors are the sharp case (long lead times and lumpy capacity give a loop BOTH a long window AND a strong incentive to overreact, the two factors the bound multiplies) and pre-frames 8.2's non-result; 2.4 makes the complexity-to-persistence translation explicit and labels the convergence a coherence check, not a tested claim; 4.6 explains the speed limit before restating it and separates "where stability is lost today" from "how much margin to leave for change"; 7.5 names all four boundaries with mechanisms, including that the source's headline chain-length crossover was a reading through noise (>half its grid unresolved at its seed count) and that the drift result FORECLOSES better estimation as a fix, since an oracle handed the true parameter fails too; 8.2 leads with the hypothesis then explains NON-SEVERE BY CONSTRUCTION (the data never visits the stable side the knee needed as contrast) - neither refutation nor null; the Conclusion becomes an argument rather than a list of ids and closes by applying the thesis to the paper's OWN dashboard; Appendix A states that the generated registry is authoritative over the summary table and that reproducibility anchors to the pinned snapshot rather than a live re-fetch (several sources are revisable); Appendix F states the three conventions once - paired comparisons, full-grid reporting, explicit resolution - and notes the last is what exposed the source's crossover, applied to this paper's own cells with equal force. **DEFECT FOUND AND FIXED - MINE, and instructive about the limits of the migration's own asserts:** the renumber regex for bare section references used a negative lookahead on a following period so it would not corrupt "Section 7.1" - but that ALSO skipped SENTENCE-ENDING references. One survived: Section 7.1 carried "...shapes everything in Section 7", a self-reference that should read Section 8 (The CHIPS Act). The migration script's MD5 asserts could not catch it, because they verify the transform reproduced WHAT I TESTED, not that what I tested was CORRECT - an assert confirms determinism, never intent. My own post-renumber verification grep also missed it, because I required 45 trailing characters of context that a line-ending reference does not have. It was caught by READING the prose while expanding a neighbouring section. Same family as the OUTLINE map-regex bug earlier today (minor digits bumped by a pattern that matched any digit before a capital): PATTERN-EDITING PROSE IS WHERE THESE HIDE, and reading beats asserting. Manuscript 6057923f88301ce83633bb142bc35fac. **2026-07-25 - E14 IDENTIFIER RESOLUTION BEFORE THE PULL; DESIGN SECTION 19 AMENDMENT; SECTION 14 VERIFICATION RULE TIGHTENED; PLUS THE TWO OWED RECORDS (Section 18 E14 amendment, per-row design pin). PRE-PULL, PRE-RUN - nothing entered the store or SOURCES.md.** THE TWO OWED RECORDS, NOW WRITTEN. (a) DESIGN Section 18, dated 2026-07-25, author-authorized, froze the manifest extension and E14 (echelon variance decomposition) BEFORE any pull: classified DESCRIPTIVE / STRUCTURAL CHARACTERIZATION with NO verdict; severity passes because a variance ratio is unbounded above, bounded below at zero, has no ceiling to saturate and no tie structure, so even-versus-concentrated are both observable at the available n; report form is characterization with uncertainty, and overlapping intervals yield INCONCLUSIVE - a statement about resolution, never a negative finding. It exists because the Phase-4 source audit found the source's echelon decomposition was neither carried nor dispositioned (COVERAGE row 39's disposition covers the persistence estimates only), and it is the paper's ONLY chance to LOCATE its mechanism in real data; everything else is predictive association or simulation. (b) PER-ROW DESIGN PIN: the builder's old assert was len(design_pins)==1, which silently encoded "designed once, run once" - true until this amendment. Every ledger row now carries design_pin_md5 and the lock carries the sorted SET; the new assert is stricter where it matters, requiring every row to carry a NON-EMPTY pin, because a missing pin means an output was written without recording its design, which is a defect rather than a variation. IDENTIFIER RESOLUTION, AND WHY IT MOVED BEFORE THE PULL. Section 18.1 named the five E14 chain series in words and deferred identifiers to pull time under the Section-14 title-verification rule. Resolving them first surfaced THREE defects that title-verification at pull time would not have caught, so the resolution is now frozen in DESIGN Section 19 rather than deferred. (1) NONEXISTENT IDENTIFIER: the plan carried AMDMNO for durable-goods new orders; AMDMNO is not a FRED series at all; the real seasonally adjusted series is DGORDER. (2) IDENTIFIER CONSTRUCTED BY ANALOGY IS WRONG: the wholesale sector series flip one letter for their seasonally adjusted sibling (S4238SM144NCEN to S4238SM144SCEN), and applying that pattern to the total S42SMNM144NCEN yields S42SMNM144SCEN, but the real identifier is S42SMSM144SCEN - the letter flips in TWO positions. (3) SCOPE REGRESSION ON A WITHIN-FAMILY SUBSTITUTION, AND THIS ONE WAS OUR OWN: the prior session's working note recommended MRTSSM44X72USS as the revised counterpart of RSXFS. It is not. FRED's own cross-references pair RSXFS with MRTSSM44000USS ("Retail Trade") and RSAFS with MRTSSM44X72USS ("Retail Trade AND Food Services"), and RSAFS had ALREADY been rejected for including food services. Taking it would have put food services back into chain step 1 while presenting itself as a strict upgrade from advance to revised - undoing a correct earlier exclusion under cover of an improvement. A prior session's recorded recommendation is a CANDIDATE, never a CLEARANCE, including when this project produced it; same class as the E8 finding that provenance is not correctness. THE COLLISION IS STRUCTURAL, NOT INCIDENTAL: total merchant wholesalers sales carries FOUR live series under a word-for-word identical title separated only by units and adjustment (S42SMNM144NCEN dollars NSA, S42SMSM144SCEN dollars SA, M42MPCM157NCEN percent NSA, P42MPCM157SCEN percent SA), and the M3 family repeats it with AMTMVS/UMTMVS, AMTMNO/UMTMNO and DGORDER/UMDMNO. The earlier percent-units near-miss was the normal shape of these Census families, not bad luck. D1 RULED (author-ratified): retail is the REVISED survey series MRTSSM44000USS, not the advance RSXFS, because the advance subsample's extra sampling noise inflates the variance of chain step 1, and step 1 is the denominator the concentration claim at later steps is measured against, so advance data would bias the FIRST link in the direction that flatters the hypothesis. D2 RULED (author-ratified), amending Section 14: every external data identifier must be confirmed against its OWN publisher series page for EXISTENCE, TITLE, UNITS and SEASONAL ADJUSTMENT; an identifier may NEVER be accepted on a naming pattern inferred from a sibling, a search snippet, a prior session's recommendation, or a plan document; and any within-family substitution (advance to revised, NSA to SA, total to sub-aggregate) must be independently re-verified for SCOPE, because a substitution correct on vintage or adjustment can silently change what the series covers. This generalizes past this paper and is proposed to the Standard as a general note. THE EXTENSION IS TWO NEW PULLS, NOT FIVE: Section 14 already lists AMTMNO, AMTMVS and DGORDER as activity context and SOURCES.md already carries all three as hashed rows, verified 2026-07-25 at 32 FRED rows plus 12 non-FRED rows, 44 data rows total; the extension adds only MRTSSM44000USS and S42SMSM144SCEN, taking FRED rows 32 to 34 and total 44 to 46. The working estimate of five new pulls was wrong because it never checked what was already hashed. Those three are PROMOTED from activity context to load-bearing, which is a used-by change made by regenerating SOURCES.md through pull.py's manifest config, never by hand-editing the generated file. REALISED SAMPLE START RECORDED, no operator change: AMTMNO and DGORDER begin 1992-02 while retail and wholesale begin 1992-01, so the common sample starts 1992-02 in levels and 1992-03 in month-over-month changes; Section 18.2's common-coverage clause already resolves this, and the write-up must report the realised start rather than the nominal 1992-01. LEFT OPEN DELIBERATELY (Section 19.4), must be closed before any pull: the vintage policy for the three already-hashed chain series - Option A reuse the frozen three and pull only the two new, versus Option B re-pull all five at one date. Recommended A: a committed hash is not disturbed without cause and the frozen operator's common-overlapping-sample rule already truncates to the earliest common end date, which is conservative. VERIFICATION: DESIGN.md append proved byte-exact by reconstruction - stripping the inserted Section 19 from the written file reproduces the pinned original MD5 87d001aff1645c94ff13e9a28e14efa8 exactly, so the only change is the append; DESIGN.md is now 160354 bytes, MD5 77a32455f27ba0d8ba49b09fced6af76. **2026-07-25 - OPTION A RATIFIED; data/pull.py EXTENDED WITH A SELECTIVE-FETCH MODE THAT DID NOT EXIST; CONTAINER SUITE ALL PASS; ONE COUNT IN DECISIONS 60 CORRECTED. Still PRE-PULL, PRE-RUN - no data fetched, store untouched, SOURCES.md unchanged on disk.** OPTION A RATIFIED (DESIGN 19.4): reuse the three already-frozen chain series and pull only the two new ones. A committed hash is not disturbed without cause, and the frozen operator's common-overlapping-sample rule already truncates to the earliest common end date, so the vintage mixture is absorbed conservatively rather than papered over; the write-up must state that chain inputs carry TWO pull dates. THE FINDING THAT MADE THE RATIFICATION NON-TRIVIAL, and it came from READING THE CODE BEFORE TRUSTING THE PLAN: **Option A was not executable by data/pull.py as written.** The script had three modes and none did what A requires. A default run FETCHES AND OVERWRITES EVERY manifest entry - so the obvious command, python data pull.py, would have silently re-pulled all 32 frozen series and imposed Option B by accident, in the same keystroke that added the two new ones. --verify hashes in place but never fetches, so the two new files would have recorded FAILED (no file). --only fetches a single id but deliberately skips the SOURCES.md write (if not only: write_sources_md), so it cannot produce the regenerated table. This is the manifest-versus-tool gap: the design said WHAT data to add, and the tool that adds data could not add it without destroying something else. NEW MODE ADDED, generator-level per the standing rule that generated output is never hand-edited: `--fetch ID[,ID]` fetches ONLY the named ids, hashes every other manifest entry IN PLACE, and regenerates SOURCES.md across the FULL record set. Implementation is deliberately minimal and reuses pull_one's existing semantics unchanged - its flag already means exactly "hash in place" - so the two pre-existing modes are bit-for-bit unaffected: hash_in_place = verify_only or (fetch_ids is not None and src id not in fetch_ids). An unrecognised --fetch id ASSERTS rather than proceeding, because a silent no-op would produce a green-looking run that added no data. MANIFEST EXTENDED: MRTSSM44000USS (E14 chain step 1, retail) and S42SMSM144SCEN (E14 chain step 2, wholesale) added with their FRED-verified title, units, adjustment and coverage carried as generator notes so the verification travels INTO the generated SOURCES.md rather than living only in DESIGN; the rejected candidates are named in those notes too (RSXFS advance, RSAFS and MRTSSM44X72USS for including food services, and the three identical-titled wholesale siblings). AMTMNO, AMTMVS and DGORDER PROMOTED in used_by from activity context to named E14 chain roles; AMTMTI and ANDENO deliberately NOT promoted. CONTAINER SUITE WRITTEN AND ALL PASS (17 assertions, stubbed fetcher, temp store, no network, no real data moved). The load-bearing assertion is NEGATIVE and it holds: after a --fetch run, 42 of 42 other files in the store are BYTE-IDENTICAL, exactly 2 fetches occurred, and both were the new series. Also proven: --verify still fetches nothing; a default run still fetches all 36 fetchable entries; an unknown id raises; the manifest has 34 FRED entries and 45 total with no duplicates; the wrong constructed-by-analogy id S42SMNM144SCEN appears nowhere; and the E14 role promotion plus the verification notes both survive into the regenerated SOURCES.md. **CORRECTION TO DECISIONS 60, and to DESIGN 19.3 as first written.** Both stated "32 FRED rows plus 12 non-FRED rows, 44 data rows in total" and an extension taking the total "from 44 to 46". The true counts are 32 FRED plus **11** non-FRED = **43**, going to 34 and **45**. The miscount came from a row-counting expression that matched the table's own HEADER row (beginning "| id | vendor |") as though it were data, inflating the non-FRED count by exactly one. It was caught because the container suite asserted the manifest length against the number I had written down, the assertion failed, and the PROSE was the thing at fault rather than the code. DESIGN 19.3 is corrected in place with a dated correction note; DECISIONS 60 stands as written and is corrected HERE, because this ledger is append-only and a wrong number is repaired by a later entry rather than by editing the record. The lesson is the one this project keeps relearning from the other direction: I verified the code by reading it and got that right, then stated a count from a one-line grep and got that wrong. A count is a measurement, and an unverified measurement stated confidently is indistinguishable from a verified one until something asserts against it.

FULL SAMPLE n=411: retail-wholesale 1.1452 CI [0.8426, 1.9547]; wholesale-shipments 0.9287 CI [0.7742, 1.4030]; shipments-orders 2.3667 CI [1.6555, 4.0952]; INCONCLUSIVE. COVID-EXCLUDED n=387: 1.5031, 1.1018, 3.1382 CI [2.1958, 4.8467]; INCONCLUSIVE, missing by 0.03 - step 3's lower bound 2.1958 against step 1's upper bound 2.2273. SECTOR ARM n=411: shipments-orders_durable 7.0292 CI [4.3525, 13.9628]; DISTINGUISHED. The ordering step is the largest in every pass and its interval excludes 1.0 in every pass; what blocked separation was the WIDTH OF STEP 1, not doubt about which step was biggest. One step ATTENUATES (wholesale-shipments 0.93), so the chain is not monotonically amplifying - a descriptive finding in its own right. Excluding COVID STRENGTHENS the concentration (2.37 -> 3.14), which is the one place this experiment speaks to the source's structural-not-crisis claim. **THE POST-HOC CHARACTERIZATION (DESIGN 21) AND THE DEFECT THAT FORCED IT.** The pre-registered suite measured resolution at adjacent couplings 1.000, 0.976 and 0.920. The realised data sits at 0.629 / 0.768 / 0.685 (full) and 0.442 / 0.623 / 0.614 (excluded). EVERY realised coupling is BELOW the lowest value tested, so the suite's headline claim - dominance of about 1.5 recovered at n=411 - DID NOT TRANSFER. That is a specification defect and it is mine: I chose noise levels to span a plausible-looking range without reasoning about what coupling real supply-chain flows would exhibit. The consequence is exact: under a characterization design the entire value of an INCONCLUSIVE result is the accompanying statement of what magnitude WOULD have been visible, and without it the result is honest but uninformative. Measured at realised coupling with the REAL retail log-change series as driver (so serial dependence is the data's own), holding n, both upstream ratios and all three correlations at realised values and sweeping only the ordering-step ratio: **FULL SAMPLE detection probability at the OBSERVED 2.3667 is 0.00 - ZERO of 100.** The headline configuration COULD NOT HAVE FIRED at the effect it measured. Its INCONCLUSIVE is therefore the outcome we would have seen whether or not a 2.37 concentration is real, and carries essentially no information. This is the same disease found repeatedly from other directions - the rubber-stamp falsifier at approximately zero power, E5's saturated ranking, E8's bit-identical arms, 8.2's non-severe-by-construction knee - now in E14's own headline. COVID-EXCLUDED is the opposite case: power at its observed 3.1382 is 0.93, so THAT INCONCLUSIVE is a genuine near-miss and not a power failure; the reconciliation was disclosed BEFORE the run, since iid injected noise makes simulated intervals narrower than reality and therefore these rates are OPTIMISTIC, putting true power below 0.93 and shrinking the tail we landed in without special pleading. Smallest swept ratio reaching 0.80 recovery: 4.0 full, 3.0 excluded. Removing COVID improves BOTH the effect and the instrument. False-positive rate 0.00 at a planted ratio of 1.0 in both configurations, so the pre-registered rule does not manufacture concentration at realised coupling either. Both frozen artifacts re-verified byte-identical after the characterization ran.

**2026-07-25 - E14 SECONDARY CONTRAST ANALYSIS: OUTCOME A ON THE PRE-COMMITTED READING. THE ORDERING STEP SEPARATES UNDER THE CORRECTLY-TARGETED STATISTIC. E14 IS SCIENTIFICALLY CLOSED.** THE READING WAS COMMITTED IN DESIGN 22.3 BEFORE THE SCRIPT EXISTED - three outcomes written out in advance, including OUTCOME C in which the proposed repair turns out to be merely a looser rule and is declared INADMISSIBLE, named explicitly so it could not be quietly dropped if it occurred. **THE DEFECT, STATED RESULT-INDEPENDENTLY.** Non-overlap of two 95 percent intervals is approximately a 0.005-level test, not 0.05, and the pre-registered rule required it against EVERY other step. That is a general property of interval comparisons, true a priori, indifferent to which way the data went. The second defect is worse because the remedy was already in hand: DESIGN 20.3 chose JOINT resampling precisely to preserve cross-series dependence and said in terms that destroying it would give intervals wrong in an unrecoverable direction - so every resample already carried a complete vector of step ratios - and the rule then collapsed each step to a MARGINAL interval and compared intervals, discarding exactly the covariance the joint resampling had been built to preserve. The design understood covariance mattered and then ignored it. A third mismatch, recorded because it explains the shape of the fix: a characterization classified as carrying NO verdict was given a binary pass-fail rule that functions as one. **SUITE: six legs ALL PASS, both machines, including TWO watched-to-fail legs - an even chain must not separate (CI [-0.1602, 0.1318]) and TWO JOINTLY-HIGH STEPS must not separate either (CI [-0.4670, 0.5447]), which proves the simultaneity does real work rather than being a label.** RESULT, all three configurations EXCLUDE ZERO: full sample contrast 1.2215 CI [0.4422, 2.7692], P(argmax = shipments-orders) = 0.9989; COVID-excluded contrast 1.6351 CI [0.3220, 3.3513], P(argmax) = 0.9952; sector arm contrast 5.8840 CI [3.2184, 12.5370], P(argmax) = 1.0000. The wholesale-shipments step is NEVER the largest - 0.0000 in every configuration. **THE GATE WAS CHECKED BEFORE THE INTERVAL WAS READ, as committed: false-positive rate 0.00 in BOTH configurations against a 0.05 ceiling.** So the contrast is a genuine improvement and not a looser rule - it fires more on true effects and NO MORE on none. Power improvement is large and quantified: at the observed full-sample effect the pre-registered rule had detection probability 0.00 and the contrast rule has 0.35; at the observed COVID-excluded effect, 0.98 and 1.00. **THE BINDING CAVEAT, and it constrains the claim: 0.35 is LOW.** The full-sample finding fired at an effect size where the rule fires about a third of the time. It is not a false positive - the false-positive rate is 0.00, so firing is not cheap - but a single fire at 35 percent power is FRAGILE and must not be presented as a strong result. The COVID-EXCLUDED pass is the sound one at 0.98-1.00 and is what the paper should lead with. Recovery rates remain OPTIMISTIC by the disclosed iid-noise limitation, so true power at 2.37 is below 0.35, which strengthens this caution rather than weakening it. The sector arm is now DOUBLY confirmed, separating under the strict pre-registered rule AND under the contrast, but its coupling regime of 0.477 was never power-characterized so no power figure attaches to it; it stands on the pre-registered rule's zero false-positive rate. **PRIMARY UNCHANGED AND STILL PRIMARY:** e14_echelon.py and e14_echelon.json verified byte-identical after every subsequent run; the paper reports INCONCLUSIVE with detection probability 0.00 as the primary, and the contrast as SECONDARY and POST-HOC, labelled so in every location. Per DESIGN 22.3 the manuscript must state that the pre-registered rule was mis-calibrated relative to the level it appeared to imply, that this was knowable in advance, and that it was not known in advance by this author. **SECTOR-LEVEL CHAINS: CONSIDERED AND DECLINED (DESIGN 22.4), closed rather than deferred, because an unrun registered test is not a scientific asset and a promissory note with no date misleads a future reader.** The deciding reason is contamination of the registration itself: a matched chain needs a NAICS crosswalk across retail, wholesale and manufacturing, and building it now means choosing sectors while already knowing the aggregate came out at 2.37 and durables at 7.03. Also not independent of the aggregate, and a new experiment at Phase-4 close. **HONEST HEADLINE for the write-up: amplification CONCENTRATES at the ordering step - not "we proved it". Argmax 0.9989 with false-positive rate 0.00 is strong; 35 percent power on the full sample is not. Both belong in the paper.** Scope unchanged: this locates WHERE, and does NOT establish that the measurement mechanism caused it.

**2026-07-25 - RENDERER FORMAT SUFFIX {{LB-id:SPEC}} SHIPPED (commit b9bb08b, ledger 506); THE PLACEMENT CHECK GETS ITS FIRST FIXTURE; THE FIXTURE HARNESS LEARNS TO ASSERT ABSENCE; AND A REPORTED 1.00 GETS ITS DENOMINATOR. PROCESS SLIP OWNED: b9bb08b was committed WITHOUT this entry - DECISIONS must be current at every commit, and this one is written after the fact rather than quietly skipped.** THE PROBLEM: TBL-8 printed roughly fifty cells of sixteen-significant-figure floats (2.3667492379916664 where 2.3667 belongs), and it is the most-scrutinised table in the paper. THE FIX AND WHY IT IS SHAPED THIS WAY: a token may now carry a Python format spec, {{LB-id:SPEC}}, applied by the renderer at substitution time. This is PRESENTATION ONLY - the ledger keeps full precision, verify.py still ties the manuscript to the stored value, and the suffix cannot alter WHICH value is quoted. The alternative, rounding in the builder, was rejected because it would destroy precision in the LEDGER, which is the one place it must never be lost. A suffix is valid ONLY on int and float: on a string, bool, dict or null it is a hard error, because those types format WITHOUT RAISING and would silently produce something the author never intended (bool is excluded explicitly, since it subclasses int and format(True, '.2f') returns '1.00'). Two deliberate non-formats: n is already an integer, and the result/outcome strings stay bare. The identity row uses .2e rather than .4f because at -8.88e-16 four decimals render '-0.0000', which reads as a small MEASURED quantity rather than the exact algebraic zero it is. **THE CHECK I WAS ABOUT TO MODIFY HAD NO TEST.** verify.py's placement rule tested for the bare literal "{{id}}", which would have marked every suffixed token as MISSING; it now matches on the id followed by '}}' or ':'. But that check had NEVER been watched to fail - there was no fixture for an unplaced ledger id among the ten - and modifying an untested check is the worst possible moment to have no test. New fixture unplaced_ledger_id (eleven total): two ledger rows, LB-X-a placed ONLY as {{LB-X-a:.4f}} and LB-X-b placed nowhere. **AND THE HARNESS ITSELF COULD NOT EXPRESS WHAT THAT FIXTURE NEEDED TO PROVE.** As it stood, run_selftest asserted only that SOME problem contained the expected fragment - so a fixture proved a check FIRES and never that it fires SELECTIVELY. A check that flagged EVERYTHING would have passed all ten fixtures in the suite. The manifest now supports an optional expect_absent_fragment, and the driver fails the fixture if any problem contains it; unplaced_ledger_id uses both directions at once - LB-X-b MUST be flagged, LB-X-a MUST NOT be - which is what actually proves a suffixed token still counts as placed. This generalizes past this paper and is a STANDARD CANDIDATE: a fixture suite in which no fixture asserts ABSENCE cannot distinguish a working check from one that complains about everything. **A REPORTED 1.00 THAT WOULD HAVE OVERCLAIMED.** The rendered secondary panel showed the COVID-excluded contrast power as 1.00. That is a measured proportion - 100 of 100 replications - and NOT probability 1; printed bare it reads as certainty, and the denominator appeared nowhere in the paper. In a section whose entire argument is "our pre-registered rule could not fire, and here is the measured evidence", shipping an unqualified 1.00 would have undercut the honesty of everything around it. A ledger row LB-E14-contrast-power-reps now carries the denominator (505 -> 506 rows) and the prose states that every power and false-positive rate is a proportion over that many replications, so 1.00 means every replication rather than certainty AND 0.00 means none rather than impossibility - the caveat was needed in BOTH directions. Caught by READING THE RENDERED OUTPUT, which no check performs and none could: every gate was green with the bare 1.00 in place. **STANDARD AMENDMENT DEFERRED WITH A STATED TRIGGER, not indefinitely:** the selective-firing rule belongs in the Standard, but the Standard was amended to v1.9.11 hours ago and each amendment costs two whole-page replacements on a surface with no hash to verify against. Batching it with whatever the remaining-sections audit produces; the trigger is the next Standard-touching action, and this entry is the record so it cannot be lost. STATE: VERIFY GREEN all six at 506 rows, SELF-TEST GREEN at 11 fixtures, RENDER GREEN 593 occurrences / 506 distinct ids.

**2026-07-26 - VOICE SWEEP COMPLETE (box 7): 41 FLAGS, 36 WITHDRAWN AS EARNED, 5 CONFIRMED AND FIXED - AND THE WITHDRAWALS ARE THE EVIDENCE THE SWEEP APPLIED A TEST.** Method per the archived protocol's Stage 3.1/3.2 vocabulary discipline under the current Standard's voice line: full-body scan for four classes (pressure words, interpretive verbs, bombast, criticism-implying), then the MANDATORY SECOND FOCUSED REVIEW of only the flagged items, one at a time with full sentences, before anything counted. Scan yield: 13 pressure, 23 interpretive-verb, 5 bombast, 0 criticism-implying. **WITHDRAWN (36), by reason:** every theorem "prove/proved/shows" (Appendix G carries the proofs - a proof PROVES, and downgrading it would be false modesty, which the protocol explicitly rejects: the goal is scoping, not weakening); "well established" as literature-status adjective (twice); every negated bombast ("conditional, not universal", "scoped rather than universal", "do and do not guarantee"); "guarantees" for a within-model mechanical consequence (the blind period's steering); "genuinely shifted / genuinely strained" (three) as the LOAD-BEARING technical contrast between true regime state and estimator noise - E9's split IS that distinction and removing the word would blur the finding; contrastive not-merely/not-only constructions (four); registered-polarity language in Methods 5.5 ("confirms the boundary" states the FROZEN registration's own declared reading and is not edited for tone); deterministic suite-behavior "confirms" (twice); the deliberately deflationary "confirms that the arithmetic is arithmetic"; the honesty line "not that we have proven it"; monitor-behavior "confirms regime shifts rather than anticipating them" (twice - detects-after is the accurate verb and is itself the thesis applied to the dashboard); idiomatic "proved unadjudicable", "the very leverage" (Minsky's reflexivity), "cannot simply adapt faster" (rules out the naive escape), "entirely above the boundary" (factual quantifier over all bins). **CONFIRMED AND FIXED (5):** (1) "genuinely close to this paper's concerns" -> "close to" (2.6 positioning; pure pressure). (2) "it simply could not see this much of it" -> "it could not see" (7.1; the deflation is stronger without the softener). (3) "the persistence is simply too high" -> "too high" (7.2). (4) 2.5 "the literature above ESTABLISHES that systems drift" -> "DOCUMENTS" - the Minsky/drift tradition is qualitative-institutional plus case studies, and "establishes" overclaimed on its behalf; my own restoration prose from two units ago, caught by the sweep. (5) 10.3 "the COVID episode SHOWS that compound shocks are outside it" -> "ILLUSTRATES" - one episode illustrates a scope boundary; it does not show it in the demonstrative sense. **THE PRINCIPLE, recorded because it rhymes with DECISIONS 66:** a sweep that fixed everything it flagged would be the selective-firing failure in prose form - proof the scan fires, no proof it fires selectively. The 36 recorded withdrawals, each with its reason, are what make the 5 fixes meaningful. Section 7.1 was the calibration model and required zero changes. Result: manuscript 23,169 words, MD5 850638bce62c75f26a8995364c9759b9, tokens unchanged 593/506; read-back confirms all five fixes landed AND all protected uses intact (spot list: "genuinely shifted, persistent regimes", "not that we have proven it", "well established in this literature", "confirms regime shifts rather than anticipating"). Li-Dorfler-2024 gave the wrong author (Li, Z.) and a paraphrased title with venue "Working paper" - the paper is Sarah H. Q. Ahn-Tan-2025's title was transposed (the IMF WP 2025/102 is "Supply Chain Diversification and Resilience"); corrected, with Tan's middle initial. Saricioglu-2025 was listed as a working paper with a paraphrased title; it is published - International Journal of Industrial Engineering: Theory, Applications and Practice 32(3), 751-769, "Impact of COVID-19 on the Bullwhip Effect Across U.S. Industries" - and the first author's initial was wrong (P. for A.); corrected. Alfaro-2025 was accurate and gains its WP number (33754). **TIER-2 SUPPORT FAILURE (1), the more serious class:** Section 8.4 asserted that "recent work on industrial-policy financing raises the same composition question for semiconductor programs specifically [@Alfaro-2025; @Ahn-Tan-2025]" - but neither source is about industrial-policy financing and neither is semiconductor-specific: Alfaro et al. study bank credit and supplier-search costs during the 2018-19 US-China tariff episode, and Ahn and Tan model import-source diversification and the resilience-efficiency trade-off. The sentence is rescoped to what the sources actually support (bank credit relationships and diversification choices shaping which supplier links firms can form and where). The exploratory framing and the no-ledgered-quantity disclaimer around it are unchanged. **PROSE VERIFIED AGAINST THE REAL SOURCES:** the body's substantive characterizations of Leng, Li-Dorfler, Saricioglu and Ning all match the actual papers - notably the Leng passage describes hump-shaped persistence profiles amplifying along the chain with an incomplete-information filtering variant, which is NBER 33638's actual model, so the fabricated title was a reference-list defect only and no argument or non-novelty concession breaks. Zero ledger impact; token census unchanged. TRANCHE 1 HIT RATE 6/10 justifies checking all 73; the sweep continues in tranches with a commit per tranche.

**2026-07-28 - CITATIONS ACCURACY PASS, TRANCHE 19 (52 of 73 checked cumulative): THE ARMA ORDER-UP-TO SOURCE CORRECTED.** **Gaalman-Disney-2009 - TRUNCATED TITLE:** the entry stopped at "...ARMA(2,2) demand" and dropped "and arbitrary lead-times"; International Journal of Production Economics 121(2), 454-463, confirmed against the publisher record with Groningen's research portal, Cardiff's ORCA, RePEc and the second author's own publication list all agreeing. **2026-07-28 - CITATIONS ACCURACY PASS, TRANCHE 20 (53 of 73 checked cumulative): A PREPRINT TITLE CITED AS THE PUBLISHED ONE - A NEW DEFECT CLASS.** **Disney-Towill-2002 - WRONG TITLE VARIANT.** The entry read "A discrete TRANSFER FUNCTION model to determine the dynamic stability of A vendor managed inventory supply CHAIN"; the published article is "A discrete LINEAR CONTROL THEORY model to determine the dynamic stability of vendor managed inventory supply CHAINS," International Journal of Production Research 40(1), 179-204. Both strings are real and both name the same work under the same DOI - the first is the title on the authors' accepted manuscript, which is what the freely available PDF still carries in its own running footer, while the journal published under the second. Corrected to the published form with volume, issue and pages, confirmed against Taylor and Francis with Scopus, Springer and the second author's institutional list agreeing. THIS IS A NEW DEFECT CLASS for this pass and worth naming: a citation can be wrong while being traceable to a genuine document, because preprint and published titles diverge and the preprint is usually what a search engine surfaces first. It is also the class MOST likely to survive casual checking, since a reader who searches the committed title WILL find a matching PDF and conclude the entry is correct. The rule applied here and going forward: where a preprint and a published version disagree, the entry follows the PUBLISHED record, since that is what a replicator's library resolves. Zero ledger impact; 646/546 unchanged; 73 keys unique. CUMULATIVE: 53 checked, 31 defects, zero fabricated sources. REMAINING: 20 references.

Recorded because it is the mirror image of the Warburton case in tranche 15: there a secondary list disagreed and the entry held; here the PUBLISHER's own page disagrees on one field and the entry still holds, because the disagreement is between the publisher's online-first date and the publisher's issue date rather than between sources of different authority.
